All supplied links, public contact emails, and GitHub-hosted video downloads are together here. Download links are absolute so they remain usable when you copy this Markdown into another document. If a browser plays an MP4, use the player’s download control or Save Video As.

## Attribution and capture

Creator and publisher credits stay attached to each source. Videos are collected as design references at Jason’s request; no reuse license was supplied. Inclusion does not define an approved personal style or transfer ownership. Original links remain alongside the saved media.

The initial nine saved MP4s passed a complete FFmpeg decode check. The expanded capture is validated in the manifest, with the two original Dirt WebM audio-header warnings noted above. [Capture manifest](manifest.json) records source media URLs, post/video IDs, file sizes, dimensions, durations, and SHA-256 hashes.

## Visual gallery

Collected October 13, 2026. Expand a website to see all saved media. Video previews are source frames or publisher posters linked to the actual video. Images open individually; no ZIP is required.

Capture covers media exposed by these exact pages, their embedded page data, and page-specific bundles. It does not crawl every linked project or archive an entire website. Duplicate bytes share one saved file; responsive image sizes are consolidated. Original source URLs and checksums remain in the manifest.
